8a1(dkar chag): gsal bar bsnyad pa'o//zhes smra ba 'di ni gnas med do//kun dga' bos gsol pa/bdag gis bcom ldan 'das kyis kun du 'khor rnam bzhi la bka' stsal pa thos pa las na shes pa yod pas na shis bya sna tshogs pa yod la/shes bya yod pas na shes pa sna tshogs pa skye bar gsungs pas na/bdag gis 'di ltar sems te/sems kyi dngos po 'di nyid ka bdag gi sems kyi rang bzhin lags te/yul dang 'phrad pa na sems rjes su skye ba zhig kho na yin gyi phyi dang nang dang bar na'ang med do//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/khyod kyis shes bya yod pas shes pa sna tshogs pa 'byung la yul dang 'phrad pa na sems/rjes su skye bar smra na de lta na shes pa dngos po med pa nyid kyis ji ltar yul dang 'phrad par 'gyur/ci ste shes pa dngos po med pa ni yul dang 'phrad par smra na yul bdun pa dang /khams bcu dgu pa nyid 'phrad pa zhig tu 'gyur la don 'di ni med pa nyid do//'on te shes pa dngos po yod pa nyid na khyod kyi lag pas rang gi lus la reg pa nyid na rtogs pa'i sems de rkang nas 'byung ba nyid dam/phyi rol nas 'jug pa zhig yin grang /nang nas 'byung ba zhig na yang lus kyi nang nyid gsal bar 'gyur ro//'on te phyi rol nas 'jug pa zhig na sngon du rang gi bzhin gsal bar 'gyur ro//kun dga' bos gsol pa/mig nyid kyis mthong bar 'gyur la shes pas ni rtogs par 'gyur mod kyi mig nyid kyis ma yin la/shes pa kho nas mthong ba yang gnas ma yin no//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/gal te mig nyid kyis mthong bar nus pa nyid na khyod nyid khang pa'i nang du 'jug pa'i sgo nyid kyis kyang mthong bar 'gyur ram/shi ba'i ro la yang mig gi dbang po snang bas des kyang mthong bar rigs la/des kyang mthong na ni ji ltar shi ba nyid 
8b1(dkar chag): yin/kun dga' bo/gal te rnam par shes pa gsal zhing rig pa dngos por smra ba nyid na dngos po ngo bo gcig bzhig gam/'on te du ma zhig yin grang /da lta khyod kyi lus la khyab pa nyid dam/ma khyab pa'i dngos po zhig yin grang /gal 
8b2(dkar chag): te dngos po gcig bzhig na lag pas yan lag gcig la reg pa na yan lag bzhi po thams cad kyis myong bar rigs la/thams cad kyis myong na ni reg pa nyid mi 'thad par 'gyur ro//reg pa yod na ni ngo bo gcig pa mi 'grub bo//gal te du ma 
8b3(dkar chag): zhig yin na ni yang gang zag rgyud du mar 'gyur la/de lta na gang zhig khyod kyi yin par 'gyur/gal te khyab ba zhig yin na ni sngon du reg pa'i tshe brjod pa dang mtshungs la/gal te ma khyab pa zhig yin na ni khyod kyi dus gcig char mgo bo dang rkang 
8b4(dkar chag): pa la reg pa'i cha mgo bos ni myong bar 'gyur la/rkang pas ni mi myong bar 'gyur ba zhig na khyod kyi 'di mi 'thad pa zhig go//des na 'di ltar rtogs par bya ste//khyod kyis yul dang 'phrad pa na sems rjes su skye bar smras pa 'di ni gnas med do//
8b5(dkar chag): kun dga' bos gsol pa/ston pa/bdag gis bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jam dpal la sogs pa'i rgyal ba'i sras rnams la yang dag pa ji lta ba 'chad pa'i tshe/ston pas/shes pa nang na yang ma yin/phyi rol na yang ma yin/zhes kyang 
8b6(dkar chag): gsungs pa thos pa las na/bdag gis 'di ltar bsams te/nang na gsal bar bya ba'ang ma mchis la/phyi rol du phan tshun gsal ba yang ma mchis la/nang du gsal bar byed pa ma mchis pas nang du yod par mi 'grub la/lus sems 
8b7(dkar chag): 'brel bas na phyi rol du yang mi 'grub pas/'di ltar na nang du yang gsal bar ma mchis pas dbus nyid na mchis par 'os so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/khyod kyis dbus nyid du smras pa de dbus nyid yin na gsal bar gnas 
9a1(dkar chag): pa nyid du mngon pas na khyod kyi dbus nyid gang zhig na mchis/gnas ga ge mo zhig gam/lus nyid na gnas grang /khyod kyi dbus nyid ston ci na gal te lus nyid nang du gnas pa lta na/de bas na logs shig tu gnas pas dbus nyid ma yin te dbus nyid kyi nang du na lus kyi nang du gnas pa dang mtshungs pa nyid do//'on te gnas shig na yod na ni drang thig yod pa zhig gam/drang thig med pa zhig grang /drang thig med pa zhig na gtan nas med pa dang mtshungs so//yod na ni yang drang thig ma nges pa nyid de/ci'i slad du na/dper na/mi 'ga' zhig gis drang thig bslad nas/drang thig dbus su byas te shar nas bltas pa'i tshe nub kyi ngos su 'gyur la/lho nas bltas pa'i tshe byang gi ngos su 'gyur bas drang thig 'chol ba nyid kyis sems kyang 'chol bar 'gyur ro//kun dga' bos gsol ba/bdag gis smras pa'i dbus ni de lta ma lags te/ston pas mig dang gzugs 'dus pa las mig gi rnam par shes pa 'byung ngo gsungs pa las na mig gis ni bye brag 'byed par 'gyur la/gzugs ni shes pa dang bral ba ste rnam par shes pa ni de'i dbus nyid nas 'byung bas 'di ni sems kyi gnas so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/khyod kyis dbang po dang yul gyi dbus su sems gnas par blta na dbang po yul dang lhan cig pa'am/lhan cig pa ma yin pa zhig yin grang /lhan cig pa nyid na dbang po dang yul 'tshol bar 'gyur te/dbang pos ni rtogs la yul gyis ni ma yin pas/tha dad pa nyid du zad pas na/ji ltar lhan cig pa'i dbus su 'gyur ro//'on te gnyis ka lhan cig pa ma yin na ni rtogs pa dang mi rtogs pa med pa nyid kyis dngos po rang bzhin med pa nyid du 'gyur bas dbus na gnas pa ji ltar 'thad par 'gyur/des na 'di ltar rtogs par bya ste/dbus na 
9b1(dkar chag): gnas par smra ba 'di ni gnas med do//kun dga' bos gsol pa/ston pa/bdag gis sngon bcom ldan 'das/maung+gal gyi bu chen po dang /ra ba 'byor dang gang po dang shA ri'i bu ste/nyan thos chen po bzhi dang lhan cig chos 
9b2(dkar chag): kyi 'khor lo bskor ba mthong ba na rtogs cing shes la bye brag byed pa'i sems kyi rang bzhin nang du yang mi gnas/phyi rol na yang ma yin/dbus na'ang med la/gnas med ba swa na thams cad du thogs pa med do zhes rtag tu gsungs pa thos pa 
9b3(dkar chag): las na/'di ni sems kyi gnas lags te/thams cad du thogs pa medapa 'di nyid ka sems kyi rang bzhin du bsnyad par bgyi'am/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/khyod kyi rnam par shes pa gsal zhing rig pa'i rang bzhin 
9b4(dkar chag): gnas med par smra na/'jig rten na bar snang dang chu dang skam la ljing zhing g.yo ba dang brtan pa'i dngos po thams cad kyi rnam pa 'di la thams cad ces bsnyad pa ste/khyod kyi thogs pa med pa 'di la gnas yod pa zhig gam med pa zhig yin grang /gnas 
9b5(dkar chag): med pa zhig na ni rus sbal gyi spu dang ri bong gi rwa dang mtshungs pas ji ltar na yang thogs pa med par smra bar byed/'on te thogs pa med pa 'di yod pa zhig na ni yod pas na ji ltar yang med par gdags par bya rigs/dngos po med pa ni med pa nyid yin 
9b6(dkar chag): la med pa ma yin pa ni dngos po yod pa nyid do//dngos po yod pa nyid ni sngod lugs su yod pa ste/ji ltar na thogs pa med par gdags/'di ltar rtogs par bya ste/thams cad du thogs pa med pa nyid sems kyi rang bzhin du smras pa 'di ni 
9b7(dkar chag): gnas med do//de'i tshe kun dga' bos dge 'dun gyi dbus su stan las langs te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas thal mo sbyar te gus pas btang nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//
10a1(dkar chag): @#//bdag ni bcom ldan 'das kyi chu bo tha chung lags te/bcom ldan 'das kyis thugs brtse bas rjes su bzung ste/da lta rab du byung nas kyang snying du sdug pa nyid glo ba la bcags shing mang du thos pa lags kyang zag pa zad par 
10a2(dkar chag): ma gyur pas/ka pi la'i rig sngags bcom zhing gzhig par ma nus pas de'i dbang du gyur nas smad 'tshong ma'i gnas su 'khrid par gyur pa lags te/yang dag pa'i mtha' la 'jug pa'i tshul rtogs par ma gyur pas/bcom ldan 'das kyi bzhugs 
10a3(dkar chag): byams pa dang brtse ba chen pos bdag cig rnams la shas tha ting nge 'dzin gyi lam rjes su bstan du gsol/log srid can rnams drang du gsol/log par lta ba gzhom du gsol/zhes gsol nas yan lag lngas sa la gtugs te

10a6(dkar chag): gcig tu mnyam par gyur to//zhing de dag thams cad na yang byang chub sems dpa' chen po ji snyed cig yod pa thams cad kyang rang rang gi zhing na 'khor bzhin du thal mo sbyar te chos ston pa la mnyan par sems shing 'dug go//bcom ldan 'das kyis kun dga' bo

10b1(dkar chag): zhing nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang /gzhan yang mu stegs byed dang /lha dang bdud kyi ris kyi dbang po dang /bdud kyi sde thams cad du 'jug go//de ni rgyud kyi rnam grangs gnyis mi shes pas/gnyan du 'khrul pa la zhugs pa dag/phye ma

10b4(dkar chag): bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams/rnam 'gyur thams cad bskyed par byed pa/rang gi ngo bo rnyed par dka' ba ste/sems can rnams kyis rang gi ngo bo yongs su stor bas na/nyin bzhin du 'bad par byas kyang phyin ci log gi rigs sna 
10b5(dkar chag): tshogs su don med par 'khyams par gyur te rang gi rang bzhin ma rtogs so//kun dga' bo/da ste ting nge 'dzin gyi lam gyis skye 'chi'i rang bzhin spong bar 'dod na 'di khyod la dri bar bya'o zhes bka' stsal te/de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gser gyi mdog can 
10b6(dkar chag): khu tshur mdzad nas brkyang ste/kun dga' bo la bka' stsal pa/khyod kyis 'di mthong ngam/kun dga' bos gsol ba/mthong lags so//bka' stsal pa/ji ltar mthong ba yin/gsol ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i khu tshur gser 
10b7(dkar chag): gyi mdog can gyis bdag gi yid dang mig snang bar byas lags so//bka' stsal ba/khyod kyis ci zhig gis mthong /gsol ba/bdag cag 'khor 'dus pas mig gis mthong lags so//bka' bstsal pa/khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs 
11a1(dkar chag): @#//pa'i khu tshur gser gyi mdog can gyis khyod kyi mig dang yid snang bar byas par smra na khyod kyi mig gis mthong mod kyi/khyod kyi sems gang zhig bdag gi phyag gis snang bar bgyi ba'i yul du 'gyur/kun dga' bos gsol ba/bcom

11b1(dkar chag): nyid ma yin par gsungs te/bdag dngangs par ma zad nyan thos 'di dag kyang the tshom med pa ma mchis so//thugs rje chen pos bdag cag gi rmongs pa bsal du gsol/de'i tshe ston pas kun dga' bo/nyan thos kyi 'khor 'dus pa dang bcas par 
11b2(dkar chag): thams cad kyang mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob par bya ba'i phyir yongs su 'chad par mdzad pas/bcom ldan 'das seng ge'i khri la bzhugs pa nyid kyis kun dga' bo'i spyi bor phyag bzhag ste bka' stsal pa/bcom ldan 'das kyis kun du 'chad pa na/chos 
11b3(dkar chag): thams cad kyi 'byung ba ni/sems las 'byung ba ste/'jig rten na rdul phra rab yan chad kyi rgyu dang 'bras bu thams cad kyang sems kyi rgyu las dngos por red par bshad de/kun dga' bo 'jig rten na yod pa'i chos thams cad kyi nang na rtswa dang lo ma dang 
11b4(dkar chag): snal ma dang 'jur ma dud yan chad kyang rgyu'i rnam pa dang bcas pa ste/stong pa'i ngo bo dag kyang ming dang rnam pa dang bcas so//'od gsal ba'i sems rmad du byung ba rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i sems kyi khams ni ji ltar na dngos po med par 'gyur/gal te khyod 
11b5(dkar chag): kyis ci nas rnam par 'byed pa rtogs cing dpyod pa'i sems kun du gsal ba 'di nyid ka sems kyi bdag nyid du mngon par zhen na/de lta na gzugs dang sgra dang dri dang ro dang reg bya sogs las su bya ba'i yul thams cad dang bral ba'i gzhan du sems kyi dngos 
11b6(dkar chag): po yongs su rdzogs pa zhig yod par 'gyur ro//da lta khyod kyis bcom ldan 'das kyi chos 'chad pa nyan pa na rnam par 'byed pa'i rtog pa 'di ni chos kyi sgra nyid las 'byung zhing /phyi rol gyi gzugs dang sgra yongs su chad pa'i zhi ba'i sems kho nar yongs su

12b1(dkar chag): gzhan gyis zas bzang por bsnyad kyang ro ma myong ba bzhin no//ston pa/bdag cag ni da lta sgrib pa gnyis kyis bcings pas/sems kyi de kho na nyid rtogs par ma gyur lags te/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag mgon med skyabs med rnams 'thugs brtse ba 
12b2(dkar chag): sems kyi de kho na nyid gsal bar mdzad cing chos kyi mig 'byed par mdzad du gsol/ces gsol pa dang /de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs kyi dpal be'u las 'od zer gyi tshogs kha dog brgya stong la byug pa spros te/phyogs bcu'i rdul phra rab snyed kyi 
12b3(dkar chag): sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad dus gcig tu bskor te/zhing bkod pa de dag gi sangs rgyas kyi gtsug tor la reg nas slar 'khyil te/kun dga' bo dang 'khor 'dus pa rnams kyang snang bar byas so//kun dga' bo la bka' stsal pa/bdag gis khyod

12b6(dkar chag): thag rin po che'i ri bo dang mtshungs pa dag cing gsal bar 'khrungs pas na 'od zer gyis rgyas lags te/bdag cag gi mig lam du snang ba na/phyag sor gyi 'khor lo rnam par bzlums te ston par mdzad pa las na khu tshur gyi rnam par snang ngo //bcom

13a1(dkar chag): @#//kyi phyag ma mchis na bcom ldan 'das kyi khu tshur du mi 'grub la/khyod kyi mig ma mchis na khyod kyi mthong bar mi 'grub ste/khyod kyi mig gi dbang po bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag dang dper bgyis pa na 'dra bar mchis sam/kun 
13a2(dkar chag): dga' bos gsol ba/de de bzhin te/ston pa/bdag gi mig gi dbang po dang bral na ni bdag gis mthong bar mi 'grub ste//bdag gi mig gi dbang bo bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag nga dper bgyis pa ni don mtshungs pa lags so//bcom 
13a3(dkar chag): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/khyod kyis 'dra bar bsnyad pa ni/don 'dra ba ma yin te/ci'i phyir na/lag pa med pa'i mi la khu tshur nyid med par rigs te/dper na mig med pa'i mis mthong ba rnam pa thams cad du med par mi rung ngo //ci'i slad

24b3(dkar chag): 'brel bar 'byung ba ma yin na/ci'i phyir bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la mthong ba'i rang bzhin la/rkyen bzhi 'di lta ste/stong pa la brten pa dang /snang ba la brten pa dang /sems la brten pa dang /mig la brten par bka' stsal pa'i don ci lta bu lags/'di skad ces 
24b4(dkar chag): gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/kun dga' bo/bdag gis 'jig rten gyi rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid thams cad bka' stsal pa ni yang dag pa'i don ma yin no//kun dga' bo ngas khyod la slar yang 'dri'o//
24b5(dkar chag): 'jig rten gyi skye bo thams cad kyis bdag gis mthong bar nus so zhes smra mod kyang /ji ltar na mthong la ji ltar na mi mthong zhes bka' stsal pa dang /kun dga' bos gsol pa/'jig rten gyi skye bo dag nyi ma dang zla ba dang sgron me'i 'od la 
24b6(dkar chag): brten nas dngos po sna tshogs mthong bas na mthong ba zhes bya la/gal te 'od gsal ba'i snang ba gsum po 'di med na mthong bar mi nus so//kun dga' bo/gal te snang ba med pa'i tshe mi mthong zhes na mun pa nyid kyang mi mthong bar rigs so//
24b7(dkar chag): gal te nges par mun pa mthong na 'di la snang ba med ces bya bar rigs kyi/ji ltar na mthong ba med ces smra/kun dga' bo gal te mun pa'i dus su snang ba'i mtshan ma mi mthong zhes tha snyad 'dogs na/da lta snang ba'i dus su mun pa'i mtshan 
25a1(dkar chag): @#//ma mi mthong ba'i phyir yang mi mthong ba nyid ces tha snyad 'dogs par rigs so//'di ltar na mi mthong ba zhes bya ba 'di ni mtshan ma gnyis ka la 'jug par rigs so//gal te mtshan ma gnyis yod na gcig gis 
25a2(dkar chag): gcig phan tshun go skabs 'phrog par gyur la/khyod kyi mthong ba'i rang bzhin 'di nyid re zhig kyang med par gyur pa ma yin no//'di ltar na gnyis po thams cad la mthong ba zhes bya bar shes par bya ste/ji ltar mi mthong bar 'gyur/de'i phyir 
25a3(dkar chag): kun dga' bo khyod kyis 'di ltar rig par bya ste/snang ba mthong ba'i tshe mthong ba de snang ba ma yin/mun pa mthong ba'i tshe mthong ba der mun pa ma yin/stong pa mthong ba'i tshe mthong pa de stong pa ma yin/bem po mthong ba'i tshe mthong ba da

25a7(dkar chag): 'brel bar 'byung ba dang/rang byung dang /'phrad pa dang /ltos pa'i mtshan nyid dang /'phrad pa dang ltos pa ma yin pa'i mtshan nyid la sogs pa bka' stsal pa rnams bdag cag gi sems kyis gsal bar ma rtogs te/da lta gzhan 
25b1(dkar chag): yang mthong bya mthong ba ni mthong ba min pa'o zhes thos pas ches cher 'khyams pa dang rmongs pa rgyas par gyur to//thugs rje chen pos rjes su brtse bar dgongs te bdag cag thams cad la dag la gsal zhing rig pa'i sems gsal bar phye ste bstan du 
25b2(dkar chag): gsol zhes zhus nas dus shing mig nas mchi ma phyung ste gdung ba'i yid kyis spyi bos phyag 'tshal nas bcom ldan 'das kyi rmad du byung ba'i bka' nod cing 'dug go//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis kun dga' bo dang bcas pa'i 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams 
25b3(dkar chag): la thugs brtse bar dgongs te gzungs chen po dang ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod pa rgya chen po mchog tu gyur pa spungs pa'i lam rgyas par bka' stsal bar dgongs te kun dga' bo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal pa/kun dga' bo khyod kyis nan tan du sbyangs pas mang 
25b4(dkar chag): du thos par gyur mod kyi/don 'di zab cing phra ba'i sgom byung gis ji lta ba bzhin du ma rtogs so//da lta khyod kyis legs par nyon cig/ngas khyod la gsal bar phye ste bstan par bya'o//der ma zad yang smad ma'i dus kyi sems can 
25b5(dkar chag): so so'i skye bo rnams byang chub kyi go 'phang la dgod cing bzhag pa'i phyir du'o//kun dga' bo sems can ji snyed bde thams cad 'khor bar 'khor ba ni phyin ci log rnam pa gnyis so sor 'byed pa'i mthong ba rdzun pa'i rkyen gyi dbang gis skye ba blangs te/las 
25b6(dkar chag): kyi dbang gis 'khor bar 'khor ro//phyin ci log gi mthong ba gnyis gang zhe na/gcig ni sems can thams cad kyi so so'i las kyi phyin ci log gi mthong ba dang /cig shos ni sems can thams cad gyi spyi mthun gyi las kyi phyin ci log gi ma mthong 
25b7(dkar chag): ba'o//so so'i las kyi mthong ba gang zhe na/kun dga' bo/dper na 'jig rten na mi zhig nad kyis mig dmar zhing rab rib tu gyur te mtshan mo sgron me'i 'od la bltas pa'i tshe 'od sgor zlum po kha dog lnga pa rim par bkra ba mthong ba lta bu'o//khyod 
26a1(dkar chag): @#//ci snyam du sems/mtshan mo 'bar ba'i sgron me'i 'od las byung ba'i 'od sgor zlum po 'di sgron me'i gzugs sam/'on te mthong ba'i gzugs zhes bya'am/kun dga' bo gal te 'di nyid sgron me'i gzugs zhe 
26a2(dkar chag): na mig la rab rib med pa rnams ci'i phyir thun mong du mthong bar mi 'gyur zhing /mig rab rib dang ldan pa kho nas 'od sgor zlum po mthong bar 'gyur/gal te mthong ba'i gzugs zhe na/mthong ba de nyid gzugs su 'gyur ba zhig na/mig 
26a3(dkar chag): rab rib dang ldan pa des 'od sgor zlum po ji ltar mthong /gzhan yang kun dga' bo/gal te 'od sgor zlum po 'di sgo na me dang bral zhing logs su yod na nye logs su yod pa'i yol ba dang bla bres dang khri dang stan la bltas pa na yang grib ma 
26a4(dkar chag): zlum po 'byung bar rigs sa//gal te mthong ba dang bral nas logs su yod na mig gis mthong ba ma yin par 'gyur zhing mig rab rib dang ldan pa'i de'i mig la grib ma zlum po gang nas mthong bar gyur/'di 'di ltar rig par bya ste/gzugs 
26a5(dkar chag): nges par sgron me la yod la mthong ba'i skyon gyis 'od sgor du gyur to//'od sgor dang mthong ba gnyis rab rib kyi skyon du gyur gyi/rab rib mthong ba kho na'i skyon ma yin no//mthar gtugs na sgron me'am/mthong ba zhes brjod par mi bya na/
26a6(dkar chag): de la sgron me ma yin/mthong ba ma yin zhes brjod du rung ngam/zla gnyis su snang ba nyid dang 'dra zhing /dngos kyang ma yin/gzugs brnyan yang ma yin no//ci'i phyir zhe na/zla gnyis su snang ba'i rgyu mig 'bras btsir ba las 
26a7(dkar chag): byung ba'i phyir mkhas pa'i skye bo gang dag mig 'bras btsir ba nyid gzugs dang gzugs ma yin pa mthong ba dang bral ba dang /ma bral ba zhes mi brjod do//'di yang de bzhin te/mig rab rib tu gyur pa las byung ba'i phyir da lta gang zhig 
26b1(dkar chag): sgron me yin la/gang zhig mthong ba yin zhes tha snyad mi 'dogs na/sgron me ma yin/mthong ba ma yin zhes so sor phye ste ci zhig brjod/spyi mthun gyi las kyi phyin ci log gi mthong ba gang zhe na/kun dga' bo 'dzam bu gling 'dir rgya   
26b2(dkar chag): mtsho chen po'i chu bo'i bar bar du gling stong phrag gsum yod cing dbus kyi gling chen po la shar nub kyi phyogs thams cad la rgyal khams stong phrag gnyis dang sum brgya yod do//gling phran gzhan thams cad rgya mtsho'i nang na gnas so//gling de dag 
26b3(dkar chag): la lar rgyal khams brgya phrag gnyis gsum dang 'ga' zhig tu rgyal khams gcig dang gnyis nas brtsam ma te sum cu dang bzhi bcu dang lnga bcu'i bar du yang yod do//kun dga' bo spyir 'di'i nang du gling phran gcig la rgyal khams gnyis yod pa las 
26b4(dkar chag): rgyal khams gcig gi skye bo dag la spyi mthun gyi las ngan pa'i 'bras bu mya ngan dang 'phrad pa'i sngon du gling phran gyi gnas de'i sems can la ltas dang mtshan ma ngan pa sna tshogs mthong bar 'gyur ba na/nyi ma gnyis sam zla ba gnyis mthong ba'am/yang 
26b5(dkar chag): na nyi zla gzas zin pa dang 'khyim pa'am/a long dang lcags kyu lta bu'i 'od 'khyug pa dang /gza' dang skar ma du ba can 'char ba dang /skar mda' rgyug pa dang /ltung ba dang /nyi snyan dang /'ja' zug pa la sogs pa'i ltas ngan pa 
26b6(dkar chag): sna tshogs mthong bar 'gyur ro//rgyal khams gcig pu des de ltar mthong ba las gzhan du rgyal khams cig shos kyi sems can rnams kyis gtan nas mthong ba lta ci smos/thos par yang mi 'gyur ro//kun dga' bo da lta ngas khyod la las

27a1(dkar chag): @##//lta bur gyur pa ni gang zag de'i mig rab rib tu gyur pa las byung ngo //rab rib tu gyur pa ni mthong ba phyin ci log tu gyur cing gzugs las grub pa ma yin mod kyi/de ltar na yang rab rib mthong ba ni mthar 
27a2(dkar chag): gtugs pa'i mthong ba'i skyon ma yin no//de ltos te da lta khyod kyi mig la'i dang chu klung dang rgyal khams dang yul dang sems can thams cad mthong ba rnams ni thog ma med pa nas byung ba'i mthong ba'i skyon no//mthong ba dang mthong ba'i rkyen ni

27b1(dkar chag): mthong bar 'gyur ro//de thams cad kyi las las sdug bsngal bskyed pa'i dbang gis mthong bar 'gyur ro//'di dag thams cad thog ma med pa nas mthong ba rdzun pa las gyur to//dper na 'dzam bu gling gi gling stong phrag gsum dang bcas rgya mtsho chen po bzhis brgyan pa mi mjed kyi 'jig rten dang gzhan yang phyogs bcu ji snyed pa'i zag bcas kyi 'jig rten khams nas sems can thams cad kyi bar du 'dra bar de dag thams cad zag pa med pa'i gsal zhing rig pa'i byang chub kyi sems su gyur cing mthong ba dang thos pa dang rig pa dang shes pa phyin ci log tu gyur pa'i skyon dang 'brel bas kun rdzob tsam du 'phrad pa dang ltos pa las skye ba dang 'chi bar 'gyur ro//gal te spyir 'phrad pa dang ltos pa dang /'phrad pa dang ltos pa ma yin pa'i rkyen dang gtan du 'bral bar nus na khyad par du skye ba dang 'gag pa'i rkyen thams cad 'gag cing skye ba dang 'gag pa med pa'i byang chub kyi rang bzhin du mngon par rdzogs nas dag cing gsal ba'i sems kyi rang bzhin dang rig pa'i rang bzhin gtan du gnas par 'gyur/kun dga' bo//khyod sngon gdod ma'i rigs pa rmad du byung ba gsal ba'i rang bzhin ni rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba ma yin rang byung ba'i mtshan nyid can ma yin pa rig na'ang gdod ma'i rig pa 'di nyid 'phrad pa dang ltos pa la byung ba yang ma yin/'phrad pa dang ltos ba las ma byung ba yang ma yin pa gsal bar ma shes so//kun dga' bo//ngas da lta slar yang yul mngon sum pa las brtsams te khyod la 'dri bar bya'o//khyod da lta 'jig rten thams cad kyi log par rtog pa 'phrad pa dang ltos pa sogs pa rten cing 'brel bar 'gyur ba'i mtshan nyid du bzung nas rang nyid kyis rnam par rtog cing byang chub kyi sems rdzogs pa ni 'phrad pa dang ltos pa las bskrun pa'i yin snyam du sems mod/da lta khyod kyi rnam par dag pa'i mthong ba'i rigs pa ni snang ba dang 'phrad pa 
28a1(dkar chag): yin snyam mam/mun pa dang 'phrad pa yin snyam mam/thogs med dang 'phrad pa yin snyam ma ma/bem po dang 'phrad pa yin snyam mas/gal te snang ba dang 'phrad pa yin zhe na/lta spyod kyis snang ba mthong ba'i tshe snang ba

28a7(dkar chag): ma yin gyi snang ba la ltos pa yin snyoms de'i tshe snang ba mthong ba ma yin pas snang ba ni mthong na ci ltar snang ba la ltos zhes brjod par rigs/des snang ba mun pa ma yin pa yang shes sla la/mun pa dang thogs med dang/bem po la sogs pa de dag kyang de nyid dang 'dra 'o//
28b1(dkar chag): de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /kun dga' bos bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das/bdag gis 'tshal ba ltar na rmad du byung ba'i gdod ma'i rigs pa 'di dang /yul gyi rkyen thams cad dang /sems dang yid dang rnam

28b5(dkar chag): pa dang snang ba'i bar yul gyi mtshams ji ltar 'byed/kun dga' bo gal te snang ba'i nang du nges par mthong ba med ce na de gnyis lhan cig mi gnas pa'i phyir/rang gi snang ba'i mtshan ma yod pa'i gnas mi shes na yul 'di gang las 'grub/mun pa 
28b6(dkar chag): dang yang med dang bem po de dag kyang de nyid dang 'dra'o//gzhan yang rnam par dag pa'i mthong ba'i rigs pa 'di 'phrad pa dang ltos pa ma yin zhe na snang pa la ltos pa ma yin zhes ba'am/mun pa la ltos pa ma yin ces bya'am/thogs med la ltos pa 
28b7(dkar chag): ma yin zhes bya'am/bem po la ltos pa ma yin zhes bya/gal te snang ba la ltos pa ma yin zhe na/mthong ba dang snang ba'i rang bzhin so sor tha dad du 'gyur ba'i phyir/rna ba snang ba dang phan tshun ltos med don gzhan yin pa bzhin du mthong ba yang snang 
29a1(dkar chag): @#//ba'i mtshan ma yod pa'i gnas mi shis par 'gyur na/ltos pa dang ma ltos pa'i don ji ltar gsal bar 'byid/mun pa dang thogs med dang bem po de dag kyang de nyid dang 'dra'o//kun dga' bo/khyod yang 
29a2(dkar chag): yul kun rdzob pa thams cad dang sgyu ma dang sprul pa la sogs pa'i mtshan ma de nyid las skye zhing /de nyid du 'gag cing zhi ba ma shes so//sgyu ma rdzun pa la mtshan ma zhes tha snyad 'dogs te/de'i rang bzhin nges par rmad du byung ba'i gsal zhing rig 
29a3(dkar chag): pa'o//'di nas phung po lnga dang /skye mi chad drug dang /gzung 'dzin bcu gnyis dang /khams bco brgyad kyi bar thams cad kun rdzob tsam du rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba dang 'phrad pa dang ltos pa'i rkyen gyis skyes pa zhes bya ba dang /kun rdzob tsam du

29a5(dkar chag): rang bzhin ma rtogs par bden pa'i rang bzhin gyi nang nas 'gro 'ong 'khrul rtogs skye 'gag btsal bas nam yang mi rnyed do//kun dga' bo/ji ltar na phung po lnga gdod ma nas de bzhin gsheg pa sangs rgyas kyi de bzhin nyid dam pa'i rang bzhin 
29a6(dkar chag): yin zhe na/kun dga' bo dper na skye bo zhig mig shin tu dag pas gsal zhing dwangs pa'i nam mkha' la blta ba bzhin du bar snang nyid las gzhan du ci yang med/skye bo de rang dga' mig 'bras g.yo med du gtad nas bltas pas ngal te 'khrul 
29a7(dkar chag): nam mkha' la nga'i sna tshogs pa tha dad pa mthong zhing /gzhan yang rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i ra'i rdzun pa'i mtshan ma zhi zhing 'byung ste/gzugs kyi phung po nyid kyang 'di ltar shes par bya'o//kun dga' bo ring ri de dag nam mkha' las byung ba ma yin/mig las byung ba ma yin no//kun dga' bo 
29b1(dkar chag): 'di ltar rig par bya ste/gal te nam mkha' las byung ba yin zhe na/nam mkha' las byung ba de nyid slar yang nam mkha' la thim par rigs so//gal te 'byung ba dang thim pa yod na nam mkha' stong pa ma yin par 'gyur ro//gal 
29b2(dkar chag): te stong pa de stong pa ma yin zhe na/rang gi ra ri'i mtshan ma de dag rang bzhin gyis spros pa dang zhi ba'i go skabs med pa'i phyir/kun dga' bo'i lus la lus de nyid thim par mi 'gyur ba dang 'dra'o//gal te mig las byung ba yin zhe na/mig 
29b3(dkar chag): las byung ba de nyid slar yag mig la thim par rigs so/gzhan yang ra ri 'di dag gi rang bzhin ni mig las byung ba yin zhen mthong ba yod par rigs so//gal te mthong ba yod ce na nam mkha' la song nas ra rir gyur ba yin na chur la mig mthong 
29b4(dkar chag): bar rigs so//gal te mthon pa med ce na nam mkha' la sod ste rab rib tu gyur na kha tshur mig la bsgrib par rigs so//yang ra ri mthong ba'i tshe mig la gdod ma nas rab rib tu gyur pa med do zhe na ji ltar na drangs pa'i nam mkha' mthong ba la ni/nga gdod

51a6(dkar chag): ba yang ma yin/'bri ba yang ma yin pa la ci'i phyir rgyu med par glo bur du ri dang chu klung dang sa chen po dang gzugs thams cad dngos su skye zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pas da lta rmad du byung ba'i stong pa dang gsal ba dang rig pa brnyes pa yin na/ri 
51a7(dkar chag): dang chu klung dang sa chen pos sogs dngos po rnams dang 'brel zhing goms pa'i bag chags dang zag pa ji ltar yang skye bar 'gyur/bcom ldan 'das kyis gang po la bka' stsal pa/dper na/phyogs bslad pa'i skye bo zhig yul ljongs zhig tu 
51b1(dkar chag): lho la byang ngo snyam du sems pa na/de 'khrul nas phyogs bslad pa'am/yang na ma 'khrul par phyogs bslad/gang pos gsol ba/phyogs bslad pa'i skye bo 'di 'khrul pa yang ma yin/ma 'khrul pa yang ma yin/ci'i phyir zhe na/
51b2(dkar chag): 'khrul pa la rtsa ba med lags na/ji ltar de la 'khrul pa zhes bya bar rung /rtogs pa na 'khrul pa mi skye lags na/ji ltar de lam 'khrul pa zhes bya bar rung/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/'khrul pa'i skye bo de 'khrul   
51b3(dkar chag): bzhin pa'i tshe glo bur du phyogs shes pa'i skye bos bstan nas shes pa na/gang po 'di ji snyam du sems/skye bo 'di ji tsam 'khrul yang yul ljongs der slar yang 'khrul lam zhes bka' stsal ba na/gang pos gsol ba/bcom ldan 'das/de   
51b4(dkar chag): lta ma lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'astsal ba/gang po/phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyang de bzhin no//'khrul pa 'di la gtan nas rtsa ba med cing /rang bzhin stong pa nyid do//gtan nas 'khrul 
51b5(dkar chag): pa med pa la 'khrul pa'o snyam du sems so//'khrul pa rig na 'khrul pa de 'gag par 'gyur la slar yang mi skye'o//skye bo rab rib can par snang stong pa lar ri mthong ba dang 'dra ste/nad dang bral ma thag tu bar snang stong pa la ra ri mthong 
51b6(dkar chag): ba med par 'gyur ro//gal te skye bo blun po stong pa'i ra ri dag nas slar yang stong pa las ra ri snang ba la skrag na khyod kyis de blun po zhes bya'am/blo dang ldan pa zhes bya/gang pos gsol ba/par snang stong pa la gdod ma nas ra ri med do//
51b7(dkar chag): 'khrul nas ra ri skye ba dang ra ri dang bral ba mthong ngo //ra ri snang ba'i stong pa la ra ri dang bral ba mthong ba ni phyin ci log yin pa la/slar yang de snang du re ba na/de nges par phyin ci log gi blun po yin na 'di lta bu phyin ci log gi skye bo la blun po'am 
52a1(dkar chag): @#//blo dang ldan pa zhes ji ltar 'drir rung /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/khyod kyis bye ste smras pa ltar na/ci'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa'i slad du byung ba'i rig pa 'od gsal stong pa la/ci'i phyir 
52a2(dkar chag): yang ri dang chu klungs chen po 'byung bar 'gyur zhes 'dri bar byed/yang dper na/gser khongs na yod pa'i gser lhang dang 'dres pa de bzhus pa na slang yang gzhan du mi 'gyur la/shing thal bar gyur pa de slar yang shing du mi 'gyur ba dang 'dra'o//
52a3(dkar chag): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byang chub mya ngan las 'das pa yang de bzhin no//gang po/yang khyod kyis dris pa'i sa chu me rlung gi rang bzhin mnyam pa nyid chos kyi dbyings kun tu khyab na chu dang me'i khams phan tshun gnod bya gnod byed du gyur   
52a4(dkar chag): nas mi 'gag gam snyam du rtog cing /yang nam mkha' stong pa dang sa chen po la sogs pa thams cad chos kyi dbyings khyab nas nges par phan tshun du chud par mi rigs so zhes smra na/gang po/dper na nam mkha' stong pa bzhin gzugs ni thams 
52a5(dkar chag): cad kyi rnam pa mi na yang rnam pa sna tshogs pa de dag mngon du gyur pa mi 'gog go//ci'i phyir zhe na/gang po/nam mkha' stong pa la nyi ma shar na snang bar 'gyur ro//sprin gyis bsgribs na mog por 'gyur ro//rlung ldang na g.yo ba 'byung la/
52a6(dkar chag): sprin dang bral na dwangs bar 'gyur ro//na bun 'thib na mi gsal bar 'gyur zhing /rdug langs na rnyog ma can du 'gyur ro//chu dwangs na gzugs snang bar 'gyur ro//khyod ji snyam du sems/de ltar phyogs thams cad kyi rnam pa dngos po   
52a7(dkar chag): rang rang gi gnas nas skyes sam/yang na stong pa la yod dam/gal te rang rang gi gnas las skyes so zhe na/gang po/dper na nyi ma shar ba'i tshe 'od de nges par nyi ma'i 'od yin na phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du khyab par   
52b1(dkar chag): gyur gyi ci'i phyir nam mkha' la nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gcig nyid la ltos/gal te nam mkha'i 'od yin zhe na/nam mkha' rang nyid lham mer 'gyur bar rigs pas/mtshan gung dang sprin dang na bun 'thibs pa'i tshe ci'i phyir 'od gsal ba 
52b2(dkar chag): mthong bar mi 'gyur/des na snang ba de nyi ma'i rkyen las byung ba ma yin/nam mkha'i rkyen las byung ba ma yin /nam mkha' dang nyi ma tha dad pa'i rkyen las byung ba ma yinazhes shes par rigs/rnam pa la dpyad na gdod ma nas rdzun pa 
52b3(dkar chag): dngos bzung med pa la stong pa'i me tog las stong pa'i 'bras bu chags pa ltar ci'i phyir de'i skabs med par phan tshun gnod bya gnod byed du 'gyur ba'i don 'drir rung ngam/rang bzhin la dpyad na/gdod nas bden zhing rmad du byung ba'i gsal 
52b4(dkar chag): ba'i rig pa de tsam mo//rmad du byung ba'i sems gsal rig ni gdod ma nas chu dang me ma yin pa la ci'i phyir phan tshun du mi chud pa dris/nges par rmad du byung ba'i gsal rig kyang de bzhin no//khyod stong pa nyid 'dod na stong pa nyid mngon du 
52b5(dkar chag): 'gyur ro//sa chu me rlung 'dod na de yang so sor gsal bar 'gyur ro//gal te yod do cog gsal bar byed na de dag kyang so sor gsal bar 'gyur//ci'i phyir thams cad gsal ba yin zhe na/gang po/dper na/chu'i nang du nyi ma'i 
52b6(dkar chag): gzugs brnyan mthong ba ltar/skye bo gnyis kyis mnyam du chu'i nang gi nyi ma la blta bzhin du gyes nas phyogs shar nub tu song ba na/so sor nyi ma'i gzugs brnyan re re rjes su 'brang nas 'gro yang /shar du gcig dang nub tu gcig 
52b7(dkar chag): gdod ma nas med la/nyi ma gcig nyid kyang ci'i phyir so sor 'gro/nyi ma so so ba gnyis yin na ci'i phyir nyi ma gcig gi gzugs brnyan mthong /zhes rgol zhing smra bar mi rigs so//yang dang yang du brtags na rdzun dang gsob kho 
53a1(dkar chag): na yin zhing nges pa'i gtad so med do//gang po/khyod gzugs dang stong pa phan tshun 'gal zlar bzung nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying por rtog pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i snyingapo nyid gzugs dang stong par gyur 
53a2(dkar chag): nas chos kyi dbyings kun tu khyab pa'o//des na de'i nang nas rlung ni g.yo zhing /nam mkha' ni rang bzhin du gnas so//nyi ma ni snang bar byed la/sprin ni mog por byed do//sems can thams cad rmongs shing 'khyams nas 
53a3(dkar chag): rig pa las gzhan du gyur te yul la ltos pas/de yis yid nyon mongs te 'khrul pa byung ste 'jig rten gyi gzugs su gyur to//nga'i rmad du byung ba'i gsal ba 'gag pa med pa dang skye ba med pas chos thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
53a4(dkar chag): snying po la ltos pa'i phyir/de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying bo ni rmad du byung ba'i gsal rig gcig pus chos kyi dbyings kun du khyab nas gsal bar byed do//de'i phyir 'di'i nang nas gcig ni dpag tu med par gyur la/dpag tu med pa yang 
53a5(dkar chag): gcig tu gyur to//chung ba las che ba mngon du gyur cing /che ba las chung ba mngon du gyur to//chos kyi dbyings las mi g.yo zhing /phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten kun tu khyab bo//sku la phyogs bcu'i mtha' med pa'i nam mkha' stong pa 
53a6(dkar chag): chud cing /sgra'i rtse mo gcig tu sangs rgyas kyi zhing snang bar mdzad do//rdul gcig gi nang du zhugs nas chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ro//yul 'gag nas rig pa la ltos pas/de bzhin nyid rmad du byung ba'i gsal ring gi rang bzhin byung 
53a7(dkar chag): ngo //de ltar yin pa la de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po rmad du byung ba'i gdod ma'i sems ni sems ma yin/stong pa ma yin/sa ma yin/chu ma yin/rlung ma yin/me ma yin/mig ma yin/rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus

53b7(dkar chag): bar rig pa dang ma rig pa zad pa nas rga ba nyid dang /shi ba nyid dang /rga shi zad pa'i bar nyid do//sdug bsngal nyid dang /skye ba nyid dang /'gag pa nyid dang /lam nyid dang /ye shes nyid dang /thob pa nyid do//

54b1(dkar chag): pa'i rgyu las byung ba yin no//gang pos gsol ba/bdag de bzhin gshegs pa'i rmad du byung ba yang dag pa'i gsal rig dwangs pa'i sems de bzhin gshegs pa dang 'dra bar kun tu khyab pa ni gzhan du med pa la/bdag gis thog ma med pa nas

54b3(dkar chag): so|/de bzhin gshegs pa la spro ba bskyed nas gsol ba/sems can thams cad ci'i phyir 'khrul pas bzung nas rang gi gsal rig bsgrib cing 'di ltar bying nas sdig pa myong ba lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis gang po la bka' stsal

54b5(dkar chag): bar 'dod nas me long la bltas te ma mthong ba na/ci'i phyir ma mthong snyam du skrag cing gdung bas ma bzod nas gdon gyi 'phrul du bsam ste myos pa lta bur phyogs kun tu gto la med par rgyu ba 'di la khyod ji snyam du sems/mi 'di ci'i phyir rgyu mtshan 
54b6(dkar chag): med par myos nas gto la med par rgyu ba yin/gang pos gsol ba/mi 'di'i sems myos pa yin gyi rgyu mtshan gzhan gtan med do//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa rmad du byung ba'i gsal rig ni gdod ma nas ldan pa'i rmad du byung

56a1(dkar chag): @#//gyi rten 'brel 'gag nas sangs na/myos bam yin pa'i rang bzhin rang gi ngang gis byung la/rten 'brel dang rang byung ba'i tshul 'dir zad do//kun dga' bo/A dzi da di'i mgo rang la yod bzhin pa/gzhi nas

182b1(dkar chag): 'dra ba'i//byang phyogs sham+b+ha la zhes bya ba'i grong //sde bzhi'i 'byor pas rab gang sbas yul der//ring min skyes nas chos la spyod par shog/ma 'ongs sa skyong drag po 'khor lo yis//grangs med dpung gis kla klo'i rgyun bcad de//rdzogs ldan gsar pa'i dga' ston shar ba'i tshe//bdag sogs 'khor gyi thog mar rjes 'dzin shog/der ni rdo rje'i rnal 'byor la brten nas//bem po kun sbyangs mi 'gyur bde ba rdzogs//sku dang thugs gnyis mi 'bral zung 'jug gi//stong gzugs phyag rgya chen po thob par shog/chos nyid nam yang 'gyur ba med pa dang //chos can rten 'brel bslu ba med pa'i mthus//ji ltar smon pa'i bden tshig 'di dag kun//'bad rtsol med par lhun gyis 'grub par shog/rgyal ba sras bcas thugs rje'i byin rlabs dang //rgyal yum sgyu 'phrul thugs bskyed lhan cig tu//'dzom pa'i bden 'bras bkra shis 'khor lo yis//snod bcud ma lus yongs su khyab par shog///!shis brjod ni/bkra shis gang zhig shAkya'i rgyal po rkang gnyis gtso//ma lus byams brtse'i bdag nyid 'gro la phan bzhed pa//bkra shis des kyang sbyin bdag la sogs skye rgu kun//rtag tu phun sum tshogs pa'i dpal la rol gyur cig/bkra shis gang zhig zhi ba 'dod chags bral ba'i chos//skye rga 'chi sogs nyon mongs nyam nga 'jig byed pa'i//bkra shis des kyang sbyinabadag la sogs skye dgu kun//rtag tu phun sumatshogs pa'i dpal la rol 'gyur cig/bkra shis gang zhi ga chos ldan thos dang dbang po dul//lha mi kun gyis mchod pa'i 
183a1(dkar chag): gnas bzhugs dge 'dun mchog/bkra shis des kyang sbyin bdag la sogs skye dgu kun//rtag tu phun sum tshogs pa'i dpal la rol gyur cig/bkra shis gang zhig mnyam med rgyal ba'i bstan pa 'di//nam yang mi nub nyams par mi 'gyur bskal par gnas//'gro kun rtag tu dam pa'i chos la spyod 'gyur ba'i//bkra shis des kyang rtag tu bde legs phun tshogs shog/bkra shis gang zhig bla ma'i sku tshe ring ba dang brtan//rnam par dkar ba'i mdzad 'phrin rab rgyas phyogs bcur khyab//bkra shis des kyang skal ldan slob bu'i tshogs rnams la//lung rtogs bshad sgrub yon tan 'phel ba nyid gyur cig/bkra shis gang zhIgs skyong 'chi med chos ldan 'tsho//yul 'khor 'bangs sogs nad mtshon mu ge'i rgud pa med//rtag tu dpal dang 'byor pa ma lus rab rgyas nas//tshogs gnyis bsags pa'i ye shes snang rab 'bar gyur cig/bkra shis gang zhe ga chos kyi phyi mo mchog 'di nyid//mi nub dar rgyas 'jig med rtag tu gnas gyur te//ma lus phyogs su chos kyi glegs bam char ltar 'bebs//bkra shis de yis sdig dang sdig 'bras zhi gyur cig/bkra shis gang zhig rnam dkar las 'di'i phan yon gyis//thub pa'i bstan pa lnga brgya tha mar 'jig 'gyur bar//gsung pa de yang rnam dkar las 'dis brtan gnas te//thub bstan nam yang mi nub dar rgyas bkra shis shog/bsod nams dar rgyas ri rgyal lhun po bzhin//
